2026-11-19T17:20:16.5853910Z [36;1m  }[0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5854229Z [36;1m  while true; do[0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5854688Z [36;1m    # Check retained logs before reconnecting; never follow a new[0m

2026-11-19T17:20:16.5859077Z [36;1m    display_pid=$![0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5859512Z [36;1m    # Completion must stop even a still-open log connection.[0m

2026-11-19T17:20:16.5860480Z [36;1m      read_result "$LEADER_TMP"[0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5860909Z [36;1m      if [ -n "$result" ]; then break; fi[0m

2026-11-19T17:20:16.5861658Z [36;1m    done[0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5861935Z [36;1m    stop_aop_stream[0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5862420Z [36;1m    # Only a finite snapshot with stable identity is authoritative.[0m
2026-11-19T17:20:16.5862927Z [36;1m    recover_result[0m

2026-11-19T17:20:16.5863594Z [36;1m      break[0m

2026-11-19T17:20:16.5869754Z [36;1melse[0m
